+# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
+# AD-243 confidence-gate teeth (the sycophancy guard must require a NAMED,
+# date-anchored GEDCOM match — not just "the architecture is consistent with")
+# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
+
+
+def test_high_confidence_gate_requires_year_distance_threshold() -> None:
+    """`confidence=high` may only be claimed when year_distance <= 5.
+
+    This is the structural teeth from Lesson 174 — a high-confidence answer
+    must be anchored to a GEDCOM residence event within 5 years of the photo
+    date, not to visual intuition the model can confabulate.
+    """
+    section = CANDIDATE_LOCATION_SECTION
+    assert "year_distance ≤ 5" in section or "year_distance <= 5" in section
+
+
+def test_high_confidence_gate_requires_subject_residence_match() -> None:
+    """High confidence requires the primary to win Round 2.5 with >=1 match."""
+    section = CANDIDATE_LOCATION_SECTION
+    assert "subject_residence_match ≥ 1" in section or "subject_residence_match >= 1" in section
+
+
+def test_all_zero_fallback_emits_literal_visual_only_phrase() -> None:
+    """When no candidate has a GEDCOM match, the prompt forces a low-confidence
+    'visual-only' admission with the exact literal phrase (so a downstream
+    consumer can detect the no-anchor case)."""
+    assert "no biographical anchoring available — visual-only" in CANDIDATE_LOCATION_SECTION
+
+
+def test_round_2_5_excludes_immigration_and_relative_residences() -> None:
+    """Round 2.5 must explicitly disqualify the two failure modes that let
+    NYC win on 02068: port-of-entry events and a relative's residence."""
+    section = CANDIDATE_LOCATION_SECTION.lower()
+    assert "immigration" in section or "port-of-entry" in section or "port of entry" in section
+    assert "relative" in section
+
+
+# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
+# Round 2.5 v2 tie-breaker (Session 167 — PROMPT-A-ITERATION-001)
+# The Session 167 Detroit eval proved the count-primary tie-breaker FLIPPED
+# 02068 to Brooklyn (3 loosely-dated matches > Detroit's 2 on-year matches).
+# v2 makes date-proximity the PRIMARY rule and excludes undated residences.
+# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
+
+
+def test_round_2_5_tiebreaker_is_date_proximity_primary() -> None:
+    """The PRIMARY tie-breaker must be smallest year_distance, NOT raw count.
+
+    Guards against regressing to the count-primary rule that picked Brooklyn
+    over Detroit on photo 02068 (Session 167 eval)."""
+    section = CANDIDATE_LOCATION_SECTION
+    assert "SMALLEST `year_distance` wins" in section
+    # The count rule must be SECONDARY (only on a year_distance tie), and the
+    # prompt must explicitly warn against picking by longest citation list.
+    assert "longest list" in section.lower() or "longest list of residence" in section.lower()
+
+
+def test_round_2_5_excludes_undated_residences() -> None:
+    """An undated 'Residence ? in Brooklyn' must not count — it inflated
+    Brooklyn's match count on 02068."""
+    section = CANDIDATE_LOCATION_SECTION
+    assert "DATED EVENTS ONLY" in section
+    assert "Residence ?" in section
+
+
+def test_round_2_5_has_date_window() -> None:
+    """A residence 11+ years from the photo date is not a match."""
+    section = CANDIDATE_LOCATION_SECTION.lower()
+    assert "within 10 years" in section or "date-window" in section
+
+
+def test_prior_confirm_requires_round_2_5_winner() -> None:
+    """CONFIRM may only fire when the prior IS the Round 2.5 winner (Codex P1)."""
+    block = PRIOR_PREDICTION_BLOCK
+    assert "Round 2.5 WINNER" in block or "Round 2.5 winner" in block
+    assert "ALL THREE" in block  # CONFIRM now has three requirements, not two

+def test_evaluate_result_grades_detroit_correct() -> None:

+    graded = evaluate_result(parsed, ["Belle Isle", "Detroit"])
+    assert graded["top1_match"] is True
+    assert graded["verdict"] == "correct"
+    assert graded["place"] == "Detroit, Michigan"

+def test_evaluate_result_grades_nyc_wrong_for_detroit_target() -> None:
+    """The Session 154 failure: NYC primary must grade as wrong for a Detroit
+    target even when Detroit is buried in the candidate list (top3)."""

+            "place": "New York City, New York",
+            "confidence": "high",
+            "candidates": [{"place": "Detroit, Michigan"}, {"place": "Dayton, Ohio"}],

+    assert graded["top1_match"] is False
+    assert graded["verdict"] == "candidate_has_answer"  # Detroit present but not primary
+    assert graded["top3_match"] is True

+    assert graded["verdict"] == "error"
+    assert graded["top1_match"] is False

   203	CANDIDATE_LOCATION_SECTION = """## Location Identification (structured four-round reasoning, AD-243)
   204	
   205	Work through the following FOUR ROUNDS in order (Round 1, Round 2, Round 2.5,
   206	Round 3). Each round must be completed before the next. Do not jump ahead to a
   207	final answer.
   208	
   209	**ROUND 1 — Describe the scene architecturally (do NOT name a city yet).**
   210	List at least 2 concrete, diagnostic visual features that could distinguish this
   211	location from other similar locations. Examples of diagnostic features: specific
   212	material (limestone vs all-glass), roofline (curved-eave vs flat vs gabled),
   213	attached vs detached glass conservatory, visible signage language/script,
   214	vegetation type (palms vs deciduous), street furniture, vehicle era/make, and
   215	any inscriptions or posters. Do not yet propose candidate cities.
   216	
   217	**ROUND 2 — Propose 2-3 candidate locations.**
   218	Using (a) the architectural features from Round 1, (b) any biographical context
   219	provided, and (c) any visible text/signage, propose 2 to 3 candidate places. For
   220	EACH candidate, list:
   221	  - `feature_supporting`: at least one specific visual feature from Round 1 that
   222	    supports this candidate
   223	  - `feature_refuting`: at least one visual feature that would REFUTE this candidate
   224	    if it were actually at this location (or "none found" if truly no refuting evidence)
   225	  - `biographical_support`: if genealogical context is provided, whether subjects'
   226	    RESIDENCE at the photo's likely date range supports this candidate. Weight
   227	    each subject's own residence over their relatives' residences.
   228	
   229	**ROUND 2.5 — Residence-Distance Scoring (MANDATORY before naming a primary).**
   230	
   231	For EACH candidate location proposed in Round 2, fill out a row in the
   232	`residence_distance_table` with these three columns:
   233	
   234	| candidate | subject_residence_match | year_distance |
   235	|-----------|-------------------------|---------------|
   236	| <place from Round 2> | <verbatim GEDCOM citations> | <smallest absolute year delta> |
   237	
   238	For each row, you MUST:
   239	- Cite each matching GEDCOM event verbatim in `subject_residence_match`. The
   240	  expected citation format is: "<subject name>: <event type> <date> in <place>"
   241	  (e.g., "Albert Fox: Residence 1917 in Detroit, Michigan, USA"). If there are
   242	  zero matches for a candidate, write the literal string `"0 subjects"`.
   243	- "RELATED" or "FAMILY" is NOT a match — only the named CONFIRMED subject's
   244	  OWN RESI / OCCU events, or one of their CHILDREN's BIRTH places.
   245	- A relative's residence (parent, sibling, in-law, spouse where the spouse is
   246	  not the photo's confirmed subject) does NOT count.
   247	- Immigration / port-of-entry / ship-arrival events do NOT count even if they
   248	  list the candidate city.
   249	- **DATED EVENTS ONLY.** A residence with NO determinable year (e.g.
   250	  "Residence ? in Brooklyn", "Residence in X" with no date) MUST NOT be
   251	  cited as a match and MUST NOT be counted — you cannot compute a
   252	  `year_distance` for it, so it carries zero weight. Drop it entirely.
   253	- **DATE-WINDOW.** A residence only counts as a match if its year is within
   254	  10 years of the photo's estimated date. A residence 11+ years away is NOT
   255	  a match for this photo (a 1934 residence does not anchor a ~1917 photo).
   256	- `year_distance` = for the candidate, the SMALLEST absolute integer year
   257	  delta between the photo's estimated date (use the lower bound of any range
   258	  you would estimate) and any one of that candidate's matching, dated events.
   259	  If `subject_residence_match = "0 subjects"`, write `"n/a"`.
   260	
   261	**Tie-breaker rules (apply IN STRICT ORDER, do not skip). The key principle:
   262	DATE PROXIMITY beats RAW COUNT. A city where 2 subjects are documented in the
   263	photo's exact year outranks a city where 3 subjects have loosely-dated or
   264	older residences.**
   265	1. SMALLEST `year_distance` wins (the candidate whose closest dated residence
   266	   is nearest the photo's estimated year). This is the PRIMARY rule.
   267	2. If two or more candidates TIE on the smallest `year_distance`, the winner
   268	   is the one with the MOST DISTINCT confirmed subjects documented AT that
   269	   smallest year_distance (i.e. count only subjects whose residence sits at
   270	   the tying minimum distance, not every residence the candidate has).
   271	3. If STILL tied AND no GEDCOM signal distinguishes them, fall back to visual
   272	   evidence ONLY (Round 1 diagnostic features) — name the deciding feature.
   273	
   274	Do NOT pick a candidate merely because it has the longest list of residence
   275	citations. A long list of off-year residences loses to a short list of
   276	on-year ones.
   277	
   278	If ALL candidates have `subject_residence_match = "0 subjects"` (after dropping
   279	undated and out-of-window events): lower confidence to `"low"` and the response
   280	MUST explicitly include the literal phrase "no biographical anchoring available
   281	— visual-only" in the `round_2_5_summary` field.
   282	
   283	**ROUND 3 — Pick the primary location AS DETERMINED BY the Round 2.5 table.**
   284	Choose ONE candidate as primary, applying the Round 2.5 tie-breaker rules.
   285	The chosen primary MUST be the candidate that wins by Round 2.5 — you may
   286	NOT override the table with visual intuition unless rule 3 (all-zero
   287	fallback) applies. Then explicitly name at least ONE other candidate you are
   288	eliminating and cite the specific visual feature OR Round 2.5 row that rules
   289	it out. If you cannot eliminate a runner-up with a specific feature or row,
   290	lower your confidence.
   291	
   292	**Subject-weighted geography rule.** When GEDCOM context is provided and a
   293	subject's own residence at the photo's estimated date range conflicts with a
   294	relative's residence, prefer the subject's own residence. Immigration / port-of-
   295	entry events are NEVER evidence of where someone lived. This is enforced
   296	mechanically by Round 2.5 — relative-only matches do not count toward
   297	`subject_residence_match`.
   298	
   299	**Output contract.**
   300	- `place` = the primary location chosen in Round 3 (== the Round 2.5 winner)
   301	- `round_2_5_summary` = a short prose explanation of which Round 2.5 rule chose
   302	  the primary, OR the literal "no biographical anchoring available — visual-only"
   303	  if all candidates were zero-match.
   304	- `residence_distance_table[]` = REQUIRED. One row per Round 2 candidate, in
   305	  the order they appeared in Round 2. Each row: `{candidate, subject_residence_match, year_distance}`.
   306	- `candidates[]` = ALL considered candidates from Round 2 (minimum 2), each with
   307	  `feature_supporting`, `feature_refuting`, and `reasoning`.
   308	- `diagnostic_features[]` = at least 2 features from Round 1 that pinned the answer.
   309	- `eliminated_runner_up` = {"place": ..., "refuting_feature": ...}
   310	- `confidence` = "high" only if (a) ≥2 diagnostic features support the primary
   311	  AND (b) runner-up was eliminated via a specific feature AND (c) biographical
   312	  support agrees (when context provided) AND (d) the primary is the Round 2.5
   313	  winner with `subject_residence_match ≥ 1` AND (e) `year_distance ≤ 5`.
   314	  Otherwise "medium" or "low". If the all-zero fallback fired, confidence
   315	  MUST be "low".
   316	- `source_type` = "visual" | "biographical" | "both"
   317	
   318	If the photograph is itself a REPRODUCTION of another photograph (e.g., newspaper
   319	clipping, magazine page, album page), say so: set `place` to describe the
   320	reproduction type (e.g., "Newspaper clipping — venue not determinable from
   321	reproduction") and note it in `diagnostic_features`. In that case
   322	`residence_distance_table` may be omitted or use a single row with
   323	`subject_residence_match = "0 subjects"`.
   324	
   325	Do NOT collapse the rounds into a single paragraph. Return your work for each
   326	round in the JSON fields below."""

   182	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

   185	def test_round_2_5_tiebreaker_is_date_proximity_primary() -> None:
   186	    """The PRIMARY tie-breaker must be smallest year_distance, NOT raw count.
   187	
   188	    Guards against regressing to the count-primary rule that picked Brooklyn
   189	    over Detroit on photo 02068 (Session 167 eval)."""
   190	    section = CANDIDATE_LOCATION_SECTION
   191	    assert "SMALLEST `year_distance` wins" in section
   192	    # The count rule must be SECONDARY (only on a year_distance tie), and the
   193	    # prompt must explicitly warn against picking by longest citation list.
   194	    assert "longest list" in section.lower() or "longest list of residence" in section.lower()

   411	def build_prompt(
   412	    variant: str,
   413	    photo_metadata: dict | None = None,
   414	    gedcom_context: str | None = None,
   415	    prior_prediction: dict | None = None,
   416	) -> str:
   417	    """Build a minimal prompt (preamble + one task section) for A/B comparison.
   418	
   419	    To isolate the prompt-structure effect, all variants receive the same
   420	    preamble, the same photo_metadata, the same gedcom_context (when available),
   421	    and the same task framing. Only the location section and the JSON schema
   422	    fragment differ.
   423	
   424	    Args:
   425	        variant: One of "baseline", "candidate", "candidate_with_prior".
   426	        photo_metadata: Dict with collection / source / filename.
   427	        gedcom_context: Optional GEDCOM-derived biographical context string
   428	            (residences, occupations, children's birth events) for confirmed
   429	            subjects in the photo. Threading this through both baseline and
   430	            candidate is mandatory: AD-241. The shadow eval is invalid otherwise.
   431	        prior_prediction: Dict with `place`, `confidence`, `reasoning` keys.
   432	            Required ONLY when variant == "candidate_with_prior" — embeds the
   433	            first-pass prediction in a refute-or-confirm block per AD-242.
   434	    """
   435	    from rhodesli_ml.gemini_extraction import _PREAMBLE, _SCHEMA_FRAGMENTS
   436	
   437	    parts = [_PREAMBLE, "## Task\nAnalyze this photograph and identify the location.\n"]
   438	
   439	    if photo_metadata:
   440	        meta = "## Photo Metadata Context\n"
   441	        if photo_metadata.get("collection"):
   442	            meta += f"Collection: {photo_metadata['collection']}\n"
   443	        if photo_metadata.get("source"):
   444	            meta += f"Source: {photo_metadata['source']}\n"
   445	        if photo_metadata.get("filename"):
   446	            meta += f"Original filename: {photo_metadata['filename']}\n"
   447	        meta += (
   448	            "\nNOTE ON COLLECTION NAMES: A collection name indicates WHO HAD these photos and\n"
   449	            "WHERE THEY WERE STORED, not necessarily where the photos were taken.\n"
   450	            "Do NOT assume the collection city is the photo location."
   451	        )
   452	        parts.append(meta)
   453	
   454	    # Genealogical context — mirrors production format at
   455	    # rhodesli_ml/gemini_extraction.py:364-369. Threaded through ALL variants
   456	    # so the A/B measures prompt structure, not data availability (AD-241).
   457	    if gedcom_context:
   458	        parts.append(
   459	            f"## Genealogical Context\n{gedcom_context}\n\n"
   460	            "Use this genealogical data to improve location analysis. The subject's "
   461	            "OWN residence at the photo's likely date range outweighs a relative's "
   462	            "residence. Immigration / port-of-entry events are NEVER evidence of "
   463	            "where someone lived."
   464	        )
   465	
   466	    if variant == "baseline":
   467	        parts.append(BASELINE_LOCATION_SECTION)
   468	        schema = "{\n  " + _SCHEMA_FRAGMENTS["location"] + "\n}"
   469	    elif variant == "candidate":
   470	        parts.append(CANDIDATE_LOCATION_SECTION)
   471	        schema = "{\n  " + CANDIDATE_LOCATION_SCHEMA + "\n}"
   472	    elif variant == "candidate_with_prior":
   473	        if not prior_prediction:
   474	            raise ValueError("candidate_with_prior requires prior_prediction kwarg")
   475	        parts.append(CANDIDATE_LOCATION_SECTION)
   476	        # Inject prior-prediction values into the refinement block
   477	        prior_block = PRIOR_PREDICTION_BLOCK
   478	        prior_block = prior_block.replace("<PLACE>", str(prior_prediction.get("place", "<unknown>")))
   479	        prior_block = prior_block.replace("<CONF>", str(prior_prediction.get("confidence", "<unknown>")))
   480	        prior_block = prior_block.replace("<REASONING>", str(prior_prediction.get("reasoning", "<none provided>"))[:500])
   481	        parts.append(prior_block)
   482	        schema = "{\n  " + CANDIDATE_LOCATION_SCHEMA + "\n}"
   483	    else:
   484	        raise ValueError(f"Unknown variant: {variant}")
   485	
   486	    parts.append(f"\n## Response Format (JSON only)\n{schema}")
   487	    return "\n\n".join(parts)
   488	
   489	
   490	def resolve_gedcom_context(photo_id: str, sb) -> str | None:
   491	    """Build a GEDCOM context string for a shadow-eval photo.
   492	
   493	    For each face in the photo, look up its identity (in identities table) and
   494	    check whether that identity has a confirmed GEDCOM link. For each linked
   495	    confirmed subject, build a curated residence/occupation/family-events
   496	    summary using the production helper at
   497	    `rhodesli_ml.gedcom_context.build_photo_context`.
   498	
   499	    Returns the formatted context string, or None if no confirmed subjects in
   500	    this photo have GEDCOM links.
   501	
   502	    Notes:
   503	        - Wraps `scripts.run_combined_pipeline.load_gedcom_data()` which already
   504	          fetches face_links + parsed_gedcom from Supabase.
   505	        - Uses variant="first_order" (matches production batch pipeline default).
   506	        - Honest fallback: if any step fails, log + return None. The caller
   507	          decides whether to send no-context or skip the photo.
   508	    """
   509	    try:
   510	        # Photo's faces
   511	        photo_faces_resp = sb.table("photo_faces").select("face_id").eq("photo_id", photo_id).execute()
   512	        if not photo_faces_resp.data:
   513	            logger.info(f"resolve_gedcom_context: no photo_faces rows for {photo_id}")
   514	            return None
   515	        face_ids = [r["face_id"] for r in photo_faces_resp.data]
   516	
   517	        # Identities for those faces. Supabase REST defaults to 1000 rows per page;
   518	        # the registry has ~4000 identities so we MUST paginate or we silently miss
   519	        # the bulk of confirmed identities (root cause of the empty-context dry-run
   520	        # observed during 154 A1 development).
   521	        all_identity_rows: list[dict] = []
   522	        page_size = 1000
   523	        offset = 0
   524	        while True:
   525	            resp = (
   526	                sb.table("identities")
   527	                .select("identity_id, name, state, anchor_ids, candidate_ids")
   528	                .range(offset, offset + page_size - 1)
   529	                .execute()
   530	            )
   531	            if not resp or not resp.data:
   532	                break
   533	            all_identity_rows.extend(resp.data)
   534	            if len(resp.data) < page_size:
   535	                break
   536	            offset += page_size
   537	
   538	        if not all_identity_rows:
   539	            logger.info("resolve_gedcom_context: no identities rows")
   540	            return None
   541	
   542	        # Build face_id -> identity record map (only CONFIRMED count toward GEDCOM context;
   543	        # mirror production's _build_gedcom_context_for_photo logic)
   544	        face_to_identity = {}
   545	        identities_dict = {}
   546	        for ident in all_identity_rows:
   547	            iid = ident["identity_id"]
   548	            identities_dict[iid] = ident
   549	            for fid in (ident.get("anchor_ids") or []):
   550	                face_to_identity[fid] = ident
   551	
   552	        confirmed_face_ids_in_photo = [
   553	            fid for fid in face_ids
   554	            if face_to_identity.get(fid, {}).get("state") == "CONFIRMED"
   555	        ]
   556	        if not confirmed_face_ids_in_photo:
   557	            logger.info(f"resolve_gedcom_context: photo {photo_id} has no CONFIRMED faces")
   558	            return None
   559	
   560	        # Pull the heavy GEDCOM data via the canonical loader (face_links + parsed_gedcom)
   561	        from scripts.run_combined_pipeline import load_gedcom_data
   562	        from rhodesli_ml.gedcom_context import build_photo_context
   563	
   564	        gedcom_data = load_gedcom_data()
   565	        if not gedcom_data:
   566	            logger.info("resolve_gedcom_context: load_gedcom_data() returned None")
   567	            return None
   568	        parsed_gedcom = gedcom_data.get("parsed_gedcom")
   569	        face_links = gedcom_data.get("face_links", {})
   570	        if not parsed_gedcom or not face_links:
   571	            return None
   572	
   573	        # Filter to faces whose identities have GEDCOM links
   574	        identified_face_ids = [
   575	            fid for fid in confirmed_face_ids_in_photo
   576	            if face_to_identity[fid]["identity_id"] in face_links
   577	        ]
   578	        if not identified_face_ids:
   579	            logger.info(f"resolve_gedcom_context: no GEDCOM-linked confirmed faces in {photo_id}")
   580	            return None
   581	
   582	        context = build_photo_context(
   583	            photo_id=photo_id,
   584	            identified_faces=identified_face_ids,
   585	            parsed_gedcom=parsed_gedcom,
   586	            gedcom_face_links=face_links,
   587	            identities=identities_dict,
   588	            variant="first_order",
   589	        )
   590	        return context or None
   591	    except Exception as e:
   592	        logger.warning(f"resolve_gedcom_context({photo_id}) failed: {e}", exc_info=True)
   593	        return None
   594	
   595	
   596	def resolve_photo(photo_id: str, sb, photo_root: Path | None = None) -> dict | None:
   597	    """Pull path + collection + source for a photo from Supabase.
   598	
   599	    Args:
   600	        photo_root: Base directory for resolving RELATIVE photo paths
   601	            (e.g. ``raw_photos/<file>.jpg``). Defaults to the script's repo
   602	            root. When the eval runs from a git worktree whose ``raw_photos/``
   603	            is empty (images are gitignored and live only in the main repo),
   604	            pass the main-repo root here so the local image bytes can be read.
   605	    """
   606	    r = sb.table("photos").select("photo_id, path, collection, source").eq("photo_id", photo_id).execute()
   607	    if not r.data:
   608	        return None
   609	    p = r.data[0]
   610	    base = photo_root or Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent
   611	    path = Path(p.get("path") or "")
   612	    if not path.is_absolute():
   613	        path = base / path
   614	    if not path.exists():
   615	        return None
   616	    return {"photo_id": p["photo_id"], "path": str(path), "collection": p.get("collection"), "source": p.get("source")}
   617	
   618	
   619	# Backoff schedule for transient Gemini 5xx (Session 154 Phase A0).
   620	# Session 153b observed intermittent 503/504 from Gemini 3.1 Pro; the original
   621	# script treated those as permanent failures, throwing away expensive prompt
   622	# context. Three retries with exponential backoff is cheap insurance.
   623	_RETRY_BACKOFF_SECONDS = (2, 5, 15)
   624	_RETRYABLE_STATUS_CODES = (500, 502, 503, 504, 408, 429)
   625	
   626	
   627	def _is_retryable_error(err_str: str) -> bool:
   628	    """Decide whether a Gemini failure is worth retrying.
   629	
   630	    We retry only on (a) transient HTTP statuses raised by the SDK, (b) network
   631	    timeouts, and (c) explicitly-empty responses. We do NOT retry on 4xx other
   632	    than 408/429 — those are the caller's fault and will keep failing.
   633	    """
   634	    if not err_str:
   635	        return False
   636	    e = err_str.lower()
   637	    if "empty_response" in e or "json_parse" in e:
   638	        # JSON parse failures might be a model glitch; one retry is reasonable.
   639	        return True
   640	    if "timeout" in e or "connection" in e or "transport" in e:
   641	        return True
   642	    for code in _RETRYABLE_STATUS_CODES:
   643	        if str(code) in e:
   644	            return True
   645	    return False
   646	
   647	
   648	def call_gemini(prompt_text: str, image_bytes: bytes, suffix: str, model: str, api_key: str):
   649	    """Call Gemini with retry-with-backoff. Returns (parsed_dict, latency_ms, usage_meta, err).
   650	
   651	    Retries 5xx / 408 / 429 / network errors with exponential backoff (2s, 5s, 15s).
   652	    Does NOT retry on 4xx (other than 408/429) — those are request-side failures.
   653	    The latency_ms returned reflects total wall-clock for the FINAL attempt only;
   654	    the err string includes attempt count for retried calls.
   655	    """
   656	    from google import genai
   657	    from google.genai import types
   658	
   659	    client = genai.Client(api_key=api_key, http_options={"timeout": 180_000})
   660	    mime = "image/png" if suffix.lower() == ".png" else "image/jpeg"
   661	
   662	    last_err = None
   663	    for attempt in range(len(_RETRY_BACKOFF_SECONDS) + 1):
   664	        t0 = time.time()
   665	        try:
   666	            response = client.models.generate_content(
   667	                model=model,
   668	                contents=[
   669	                    types.Content(
   670	                        parts=[
   671	                            types.Part.from_text(text=prompt_text),
   672	                            types.Part.from_bytes(data=image_bytes, mime_type=mime),
   673	                        ]
   674	                    )
   675	                ],
   676	                config=types.GenerateContentConfig(
   677	                    response_mime_type="application/json",
   678	                    temperature=0.1,
   679	                ),
   680	            )
   681	            latency_ms = int((time.time() - t0) * 1000)
   682	            text = response.text
   683	            if not text:
   684	                last_err = "empty_response"
   685	            else:
   686	                try:
   687	                    parsed = json.loads(text)
   688	                    if isinstance(parsed, list) and parsed:
   689	                        parsed = parsed[0]
   690	                except Exception as parse_err:
   691	                    last_err = f"json_parse: {parse_err}"
   692	                else:
   693	                    usage = None
   694	                    if hasattr(response, "usage_metadata") and response.usage_metadata:
   695	                        um = response.usage_metadata
   696	                        usage = {
   697	                            "prompt_token_count": getattr(um, "prompt_token_count", None),
   698	                            "candidates_token_count": getattr(um, "candidates_token_count", None),
   699	                            "total_token_count": getattr(um, "total_token_count", None),
   700	                        }
   701	                    return parsed, latency_ms, usage, None
   702	        except Exception as e:
   703	            latency_ms = int((time.time() - t0) * 1000)
   704	            last_err = str(e)
   705	
   706	        # Decide whether to retry
   707	        if attempt < len(_RETRY_BACKOFF_SECONDS) and _is_retryable_error(last_err):
   708	            wait = _RETRY_BACKOFF_SECONDS[attempt]
   709	            logger.warning(
   710	                f"    Gemini call failed (attempt {attempt + 1}/{len(_RETRY_BACKOFF_SECONDS) + 1}): "
   711	                f"{last_err[:140]} — retrying in {wait}s"
   712	            )
   713	            time.sleep(wait)
   714	            continue
   715	        break
   716	
   717	    annotated_err = f"{last_err} [after {attempt + 1} attempt(s)]" if last_err else "unknown_error"
   718	    return None, latency_ms, None, annotated_err
   719	
   720	
   721	def evaluate_result(parsed: dict | None, expected_aliases: list[str], special: str | None = None) -> dict:
   722	    """Grade a parsed Gemini location result against expected aliases."""
   723	    if parsed is None:
   724	        return {"verdict": "error", "top1_match": False, "top3_match": False, "place": None, "confidence": None}
   725	
   726	    loc = parsed.get("location") if isinstance(parsed.get("location"), dict) else parsed
   727	    if not isinstance(loc, dict):
   728	        return {"verdict": "error", "top1_match": False, "top3_match": False, "place": None, "confidence": None}
   729	
   730	    place = (loc.get("place") or "").strip()
   731	    confidence = loc.get("confidence")
   732	
   733	    # Normalize alias match
   734	    place_l = place.lower()
   735	
   736	    def matches_any(aliases, text):
   737	        return any(a.lower() in text for a in aliases)
   738	
   739	    top1_match = matches_any(expected_aliases, place_l)
   740	
   741	    # Top-3: also check candidates list
   742	    cands = loc.get("candidates") or []
   743	    cand_texts = []
   744	    for c in cands:
   745	        if isinstance(c, dict):
   746	            cand_texts.append((c.get("place") or "").lower())
   747	        elif isinstance(c, str):
   748	            cand_texts.append(c.lower())
   749	    top3_match = top1_match or any(matches_any(expected_aliases, t) for t in cand_texts)
   750	
   751	    if special == "newspaper_recognition":
   752	        # For a newspaper clipping, "correct" means recognizing it as a reproduction
   753	        text_blob = json.dumps(loc).lower()
   754	        recognized = any(kw in text_blob for kw in ["newspaper", "clipping", "newsprint", "halftone", "reproduction"])
   755	        top1_match = recognized
   756	        top3_match = recognized
   757	
   758	    return {
   759	        "verdict": "correct" if top1_match else ("candidate_has_answer" if top3_match else "wrong"),
   760	        "top1_match": top1_match,

 succeeded in 143ms:

   768	def main():
   769	    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
   770	    ap.add_argument("--dry-run", action="store_true")
   771	    ap.add_argument("--max-cost", type=float, default=2.0)
   772	    ap.add_argument(
   773	        "--max-calls",
   774	        type=int,
   775	        default=None,
   776	        help=(
   777	            "Hard ceiling on the TOTAL number of Gemini calls (including silent "
   778	            "first passes). The run ABORTS the moment this many calls have been "
   779	            "made, even mid-photo. Use alongside --max-cost for a mechanical "
   780	            "double cap on bounded eval runs."
   781	        ),
   782	    )
   783	    ap.add_argument(
   784	        "--no-db-log",
   785	        action="store_true",
   786	        help=(
   787	            "Skip the append-only gemini_api_calls audit logging. The eval still "
   788	            "writes its results to the --out-path JSON. Use for bounded reruns "
   789	            "where the only required artifact is the docs/feedback file."
   790	        ),
   791	    )
   792	    ap.add_argument("--limit", type=int, default=None, help="limit to first N photos (after dedup)")
   793	    ap.add_argument(
   794	        "--variants",
   795	        default="baseline,candidate,candidate_with_prior",
   796	        help="Comma-separated list of variants to run. Default: all three.",
   797	    )
   798	    ap.add_argument(
   799	        "--photo-ids",
   800	        default=None,
   801	        help="Comma-separated photo_ids to filter test set (e.g. for Detroit-only rerun).",
   802	    )
   803	    ap.add_argument(
   804	        "--gedcom-context-fixture",
   805	        default=None,
   806	        help=(
   807	            "Path to a JSON fixture mapping photo_id -> gedcom_context string. "
   808	            "When set, the script READS context from the fixture instead of "
   809	            "re-resolving from Supabase. Use this for deterministic re-runs. "
   810	            "If a photo_id is missing from the fixture, the script resolves it "
   811	            "from Supabase and writes back to the fixture so subsequent runs "
   812	            "are stable. Default: tests/fixtures/session154_gedcom_context.json"
   813	        ),
   814	    )
   815	    ap.add_argument(
   816	        "--no-gedcom-context",
   817	        action="store_true",
   818	        help="Disable GEDCOM context entirely (legacy 153b behavior — use only to reproduce that run).",
   819	    )
   820	    ap.add_argument(
   821	        "--photo-root",
   822	        default=None,
   823	        help=(
   824	            "Base directory for resolving RELATIVE photo paths (raw_photos/...). "
   825	            "Defaults to the script's repo root. When running from a git worktree "
   826	            "whose raw_photos/ is empty, point this at the main repo root so the "
   827	            "local image bytes can be read."
   828	        ),
   829	    )
   830	    ap.add_argument(
   831	        "--experiment-id",
   832	        default=None,
   833	        help=(
   834	            "Override the experiment_id used for gemini_api_calls logging + the "
   835	            "results file. Default: session154_shadow_eval_<ts>. Pass a "
   836	            "session-specific tag (e.g. session167_detroit_eval_<ts>) for honest "
   837	            "provenance so a later rerun is distinguishable from the 154 run."
   838	        ),
   839	    )
   840	    ap.add_argument(
   841	        "--out-path",
   842	        default=None,
   843	        help=(
   844	            "Override the raw-results JSON output path. Default is derived from "
   845	            "the test-set scope (Detroit-only -> session-154-...detroit-rerun.json). "
   846	            "Pass an explicit path to avoid clobbering a prior session's record."
   847	        ),
   848	    )
   849	    args = ap.parse_args()
   850	
   851	    photo_root = Path(args.photo_root).resolve() if args.photo_root else None
   852	
   853	    from dotenv import load_dotenv
   854	
   855	    load_dotenv(".env")
   856	
   857	    api_key = os.environ.get("GEMINI_API_KEY") or os.environ.get("GOOGLE_API_KEY")
   858	    if not api_key:
   859	        logger.error("GEMINI_API_KEY not set")
   860	        sys.exit(1)
   861	
   862	    from supabase import create_client
   863	
   864	    sb = create_client(
   865	        os.environ["SUPABASE_URL"],
   866	        os.environ.get("SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY") or os.environ["SUPABASE_ANON_KEY"],
   867	    )
   868	
   869	    from rhodesli_ml.gemini_config import GEMINI_MODEL, get_model_pricing
   870	    from app.supabase_data import log_gemini_call
   871	
   872	    pricing = get_model_pricing(GEMINI_MODEL)
   873	    logger.info(f"Model: {GEMINI_MODEL}  pricing(input/output per 1M): {pricing.get('input')}/{pricing.get('output')}")
   874	
   875	    # Resolve test photos (optionally filter by --photo-ids)
   876	    photo_id_filter = None
   877	    if args.photo_ids:
   878	        photo_id_filter = {x.strip() for x in args.photo_ids.split(",") if x.strip()}
   879	        logger.info(f"Filtering test set to {len(photo_id_filter)} photo_ids: {sorted(photo_id_filter)}")
   880	
   881	    test_rows = []
   882	    for entry in TEST_SET:
   883	        if photo_id_filter and entry["photo_id"] not in photo_id_filter:
   884	            continue
   885	        photo = resolve_photo(entry["photo_id"], sb, photo_root=photo_root)
   886	        if not photo:
   887	            logger.warning(f"SKIP {entry['photo_id']} — not found or no local file")
   888	            continue
   889	        test_rows.append({**entry, "_path": photo["path"], "_collection": photo["collection"], "_source": photo["source"]})
   890	
   891	    if args.limit:
   892	        test_rows = test_rows[: args.limit]
   893	
   894	    logger.info(f"Test set: {len(test_rows)} photos")
   895	    for r in test_rows:
   896	        logger.info(f"  {r['photo_id'][:55]:55s}  bucket={r['bucket']}  gt={r['expected_location']}")
   897	
   898	    # Resolve variants
   899	    variants = [v.strip() for v in args.variants.split(",") if v.strip()]
   900	    valid_variants = {"baseline", "candidate", "candidate_with_prior"}
   901	    bad = set(variants) - valid_variants
   902	    if bad:
   903	        logger.error(f"Unknown variants: {bad}. Valid: {valid_variants}")
   904	        sys.exit(1)
   905	    logger.info(f"Variants: {variants}")
   906	
   907	    # Resolve GEDCOM context for each photo (fixture-cached for determinism, AD-241).
   908	    fixture_path = Path(args.gedcom_context_fixture or "tests/fixtures/session154_gedcom_context.json")
   909	    fixture_data: dict[str, str | None] = {}
   910	    if fixture_path.exists():
   911	        try:
   912	            with open(fixture_path) as f:
   913	                fixture_data = json.load(f)
   914	            logger.info(f"GEDCOM fixture: loaded {len(fixture_data)} entries from {fixture_path}")
   915	        except Exception as e:
   916	            logger.warning(f"GEDCOM fixture {fixture_path} unreadable: {e} — re-resolving")
   917	
   918	    context_map: dict[str, str | None] = {}
   919	    if not args.no_gedcom_context:
   920	        for r in test_rows:
   921	            pid = r["photo_id"]
   922	            if pid in fixture_data:
   923	                context_map[pid] = fixture_data[pid]
   924	                logger.info(
   925	                    f"GEDCOM context: {pid[:50]:50s} fixture-hit  "
   926	                    f"({'present' if fixture_data[pid] else 'NULL'})"
   927	                )
   928	            else:
   929	                ctx = resolve_gedcom_context(pid, sb)
   930	                context_map[pid] = ctx
   931	                logger.info(
   932	                    f"GEDCOM context: {pid[:50]:50s} resolved  "
   933	                    f"({'present, ' + str(len(ctx)) + ' chars' if ctx else 'NULL'})"
   934	                )
   935	        # Persist the resolved contexts so subsequent runs are deterministic
   936	        try:
   937	            fixture_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   938	            persisted = {**fixture_data, **context_map}
   939	            with open(fixture_path, "w") as f:
   940	                json.dump(persisted, f, indent=2)
   941	            logger.info(f"GEDCOM fixture: wrote {len(persisted)} entries to {fixture_path}")
   942	        except Exception as e:
   943	            logger.warning(f"Could not persist GEDCOM fixture {fixture_path}: {e}")
   944	    else:
   945	        logger.warning("--no-gedcom-context set: running shadow eval WITHOUT GEDCOM context (legacy 153b mode)")
   946	
   947	    if args.dry_run:
   948	        logger.info("Dry run — exiting before any API calls")
   949	        # Useful side-effect: dry-run still resolves+writes the fixture above.
   950	        return
   951	
   952	    # Unique experiment ID for this run. Honest provenance: an explicit
   953	    # --experiment-id keeps a later (e.g. Session 167) rerun distinguishable
   954	    # from the Session 154 run in gemini_api_calls.
   955	    experiment_id = args.experiment_id or f"session154_shadow_eval_{int(time.time())}"
   956	    logger.info(f"Experiment ID: {experiment_id}")
   957	
   958	    results = []
   959	    total_cost = 0.0
   960	    call_count = 0  # every Gemini call (incl. silent first passes) counts toward --max-calls
   961	
   962	    def _cap_hit() -> str | None:
   963	        """Mechanical double-cap. Returns a reason string if a ceiling is hit."""
   964	        if total_cost >= args.max_cost:
   965	            return f"cost cap ${args.max_cost:.2f} (spent ${total_cost:.4f})"
   966	        if args.max_calls is not None and call_count >= args.max_calls:
   967	            return f"call cap {args.max_calls} (made {call_count})"
   968	        return None
   969	
   970	    if args.max_calls is not None:
   971	        logger.info(f"Mechanical caps: max_cost=${args.max_cost:.2f}  max_calls={args.max_calls}")
   972	
   973	    aborted = False
   974	    for i, row in enumerate(test_rows):
   975	        if aborted:
   976	            break
   977	        pid = row["photo_id"]
   978	        photo_path = Path(row["_path"])
   979	        suffix = photo_path.suffix
   980	        try:
   981	            image_bytes = photo_path.read_bytes()
   982	        except Exception as e:
   983	            logger.error(f"Could not read {photo_path}: {e}")
   984	            continue
   985	
   986	        photo_metadata = {"collection": row["_collection"], "source": row["_source"], "filename": photo_path.name}
   987	        photo_gedcom_context = context_map.get(pid)
   988	
   989	        # Per-photo cache of first-pass candidate result for the candidate_with_prior variant.
   990	        # Same photo's `candidate` run feeds the prior_prediction for `candidate_with_prior`.
   991	        first_pass_cache = None
   992	
   993	        for variant in variants:
   994	            cap_reason = _cap_hit()
   995	            if cap_reason:
   996	                logger.warning(f"ABORT: {cap_reason} reached — halting before next call")
   997	                aborted = True
   998	                break
   999	
  1000	            # Build the prompt — candidate_with_prior needs a first-pass result
  1001	            if variant == "candidate_with_prior":
  1002	                if first_pass_cache is None:
  1003	                    # If `candidate` wasn't run for this photo (e.g. user passed
  1004	                    # --variants candidate_with_prior alone), run a silent first pass
  1005	                    # so the prior-prediction has substance. Cost still bills under
  1006	                    # the same budget.
  1007	                    fp_prompt = build_prompt(
  1008	                        "candidate", photo_metadata=photo_metadata, gedcom_context=photo_gedcom_context
  1009	                    )
  1010	                    logger.info(f"[{i+1}/{len(test_rows)}] {pid[:40]} variant=candidate (silent first pass)")
  1011	                    call_count += 1
  1012	                    fp_parsed, _, fp_usage, fp_err = call_gemini(fp_prompt, image_bytes, suffix, GEMINI_MODEL, api_key)
  1013	                    if fp_err or not fp_parsed:
  1014	                        logger.warning(f"    silent first pass failed ({fp_err}); skipping candidate_with_prior")
  1015	                        continue
  1016	                    fp_loc = fp_parsed.get("location", fp_parsed) if isinstance(fp_parsed.get("location"), dict) else fp_parsed
  1017	                    first_pass_cache = {
  1018	                        "place": fp_loc.get("place"),
  1019	                        "confidence": fp_loc.get("confidence"),
  1020	                        "reasoning": (
  1021	                            fp_loc.get("round1_description")
  1022	                            or json.dumps(fp_loc.get("candidates"))[:400]
  1023	                        ),
  1024	                    }
  1025	                    # Token accounting for the silent first pass
  1026	                    fp_pt = (fp_usage or {}).get("prompt_token_count") or len(fp_prompt) // 4
  1027	                    fp_ct = (fp_usage or {}).get("candidates_token_count") or len(json.dumps(fp_parsed)) // 4
  1028	                    total_cost += fp_pt * pricing.get("input", 2.0) / 1_000_000 + fp_ct * pricing.get("output", 12.0) / 1_000_000
  1029	                    logger.info(f"    LEDGER: calls={call_count}  spent=${total_cost:.4f} / cap ${args.max_cost:.2f}")
  1030	                prompt_text = build_prompt(
  1031	                    variant,
  1032	                    photo_metadata=photo_metadata,
  1033	                    gedcom_context=photo_gedcom_context,
  1034	                    prior_prediction=first_pass_cache,
  1035	                )
  1036	            else:
  1037	                prompt_text = build_prompt(
  1038	                    variant, photo_metadata=photo_metadata, gedcom_context=photo_gedcom_context
  1039	                )
  1040	
  1041	            logger.info(f"[{i+1}/{len(test_rows)}] {pid[:40]} variant={variant}")
  1042	
  1043	            call_count += 1
  1044	            parsed, latency_ms, usage, err = call_gemini(prompt_text, image_bytes, suffix, GEMINI_MODEL, api_key)
  1045	
  1046	            # If this is the candidate variant, cache the result for any subsequent
  1047	            # candidate_with_prior pass (avoids the silent first-pass spend above).
  1048	            if variant == "candidate" and parsed is not None and "candidate_with_prior" in variants:
  1049	                cand_loc = parsed.get("location", parsed) if isinstance(parsed.get("location"), dict) else parsed
  1050	                first_pass_cache = {
  1051	                    "place": cand_loc.get("place"),
  1052	                    "confidence": cand_loc.get("confidence"),
  1053	                    "reasoning": (
  1054	                        cand_loc.get("round1_description")
  1055	                        or json.dumps(cand_loc.get("candidates"))[:400]
  1056	                    ),
  1057	                }
  1058	
  1059	            # Token accounting (prefer Gemini's own counts, fall back to char/4 estimate)
  1060	            if usage and usage.get("prompt_token_count"):
  1061	                pt = usage["prompt_token_count"]
  1062	                ct = usage.get("candidates_token_count") or 0
  1063	            else:
  1064	                pt = len(prompt_text) // 4
  1065	                resp_text = json.dumps(parsed) if parsed else ""
  1066	                ct = len(resp_text) // 4
  1067	            cost = pt * pricing.get("input", 2.0) / 1_000_000 + ct * pricing.get("output", 12.0) / 1_000_000
  1068	            total_cost += cost
  1069	
  1070	            graded = evaluate_result(parsed, row["expected_aliases"], row.get("special_verdict"))
  1071	            logger.info(
  1072	                f"    result place={graded['place']!r} conf={graded['confidence']} "
  1073	                f"verdict={graded['verdict']} cost=${cost:.4f}"
  1074	            )
  1075	            logger.info(f"    LEDGER: calls={call_count}  spent=${total_cost:.4f} / cap ${args.max_cost:.2f}")
  1076	
  1077	            # Log to gemini_api_calls — record gedcom_context presence + variant.
  1078	            # Sub-keys pass=1 (candidate) vs pass=2 (candidate_with_prior) per AD-242.
  1079	            pass_num = 2 if variant == "candidate_with_prior" else 1
  1080	            response_summary = {
  1081	                "experiment_variant": variant,
  1082	                "experiment_id": experiment_id,
  1083	                "pass": pass_num,
  1084	                "predicted_place": graded["place"],
  1085	                "predicted_confidence": graded["confidence"],
  1086	                "ground_truth_location": row["expected_location"],
  1087	                "top1_match": graded["top1_match"],
  1088	                "top3_match": graded["top3_match"],
  1089	                "verdict": graded["verdict"],
  1090	                "bucket": row["bucket"],
  1091	                "candidates": graded.get("candidates"),
  1092	                "gedcom_context_present": photo_gedcom_context is not None,
  1093	                "gedcom_context_chars": len(photo_gedcom_context) if photo_gedcom_context else 0,
  1094	            }
  1095	            if args.no_db_log:
  1096	                logger.info("    --no-db-log set: skipping gemini_api_calls audit write")
  1097	            else:
  1098	                try:
  1099	                    log_gemini_call(
  1100	                        photo_id=pid,
  1101	                        model_used=GEMINI_MODEL,
  1102	                        call_type="experiment_location_shadow_eval",
  1103	                        prompt_tokens=pt,
  1104	                        completion_tokens=ct,
  1105	                        total_tokens=pt + ct,
  1106	                        cost_usd=round(cost, 6),
  1107	                        latency_ms=latency_ms,
  1108	                        status="success" if err is None else "error",
  1109	                        error_message=err,
  1110	                        response_summary=response_summary,
  1111	                        gemini_config={
  1112	                            "experiment_id": experiment_id,
  1113	                            "variant": variant,
  1114	                            "pass": pass_num,
  1115	                            "attempt_label": f"{variant}_shadow_eval_pass{pass_num}",
  1116	                            "request_surface": "scripts.session153_shadow_eval",
  1117	                            "request_mode": "experiment",
  1118	                            "trigger": "shadow_eval_prompt_ab",
  1119	                            "preset": "location_only",
  1120	                            "temperature": 0.1,
  1121	                            "gedcom_context_present": photo_gedcom_context is not None,
  1122	                        },
  1123	                        prompt_text=prompt_text,
  1124	                        full_response=parsed,
  1125	                        experiment_id=experiment_id,
  1126	                    )
  1127	                except Exception as log_err:

  1129	
  1130	            # Capture the Round 2.5 audit trail in the file output so the
  1131	            # docs/feedback artifact is independently auditable (Codex P1 —
  1132	            # the JSON must not omit residence_distance_table / round_2_5_summary).
  1133	            _loc = {}
  1134	            if isinstance(parsed, dict):
  1135	                _loc = parsed.get("location") if isinstance(parsed.get("location"), dict) else parsed
  1136	            results.append(
  1137	                {
  1138	                    "photo_id": pid,
  1139	                    "bucket": row["bucket"],
  1140	                    "variant": variant,
  1141	                    "pass": pass_num,
  1142	                    "expected_location": row["expected_location"],
  1143	                    "predicted_place": graded["place"],
  1144	                    "predicted_confidence": graded["confidence"],
  1145	                    "top1_match": graded["top1_match"],
  1146	                    "top3_match": graded["top3_match"],
  1147	                    "verdict": graded["verdict"],
  1148	                    "cost_usd": round(cost, 6),
  1149	                    "latency_ms": latency_ms,
  1150	                    "error": err,
  1151	                    "candidates": graded.get("candidates"),
  1152	                    "residence_distance_table": _loc.get("residence_distance_table"),

  1154	                    "eliminated_runner_up": _loc.get("eliminated_runner_up"),

  1157	                }

  1159	        cap_reason = _cap_hit()

  1162	            aborted = True
  1163	            break
  1164	
  1165	    logger.info(f"Total cost: ${total_cost:.4f}  results rows: {len(results)}")
  1166	
  1167	    # Write raw results to disk. Output path mirrors the test-set scope so
  1168	    # Detroit-only reruns and full-eval runs don't clobber each other.
  1169	    is_detroit_only = bool(photo_id_filter) and photo_id_filter.issubset({
  1170	        "inbox_fox-charlie-001_204_02068_p_13akf5twbc3600",
  1171	        "inbox_fox-charlie-001_3_01659_p_13akf5twbc1045",

  1175	        out_path = Path(args.out_path)
  1176	    elif is_detroit_only:

  1178	    elif args.no_gedcom_context:

  1180	    else:
  1181	        out_path = Path("docs/feedback/session-154-shadow-eval-full.json")
  1182	    out_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
  1183	    with open(out_path, "w") as f:

  1186	                "experiment_id": experiment_id,
  1187	                "model": GEMINI_MODEL,
  1188	                "variants": variants,
  1189	                "total_cost_usd": round(total_cost, 6),
  1190	                "n_photos": len({r["photo_id"] for r in results}),
  1191	                "n_calls": len(results),
  1192	                "gedcom_context_disabled": args.no_gedcom_context,
  1193	                "generated_at": datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(),
  1194	                "results": results,
  1195	            },
  1196	            f,

  1198	        )

  1203	    main()

     1	#!/usr/bin/env python3
     2	"""Session 153 Shadow Eval — Gemini Location Prompt A/B.
     3	
     4	Addresses Codex P1 finding from session-153-codex-audit.md:
     5	  "Attempt 3 included candidate hints with the correct answer baked in.
     6	   One photo cannot justify permanent prompt changes. Shadow-evaluate on
     7	   ≥10 diverse known-location photos."
     8	
     9	Design:
    10	  - Test set: 10-12 photos pulled from Supabase where collection + visual
    11	    evidence strongly co-pin the location. Bucketed (Rhodes, Tampa, Dayton,
    12	    Fader, Newspapers.com, Detroit control).
    13	  - Ground truth: established from (a) collection name, (b) community metadata,
    14	    (c) for the Detroit control, external visual evidence (Belle Isle
    15	    Conservatory). Explicitly NOT from Gemini's own prior output on the same
    16	    photo.
    17	  - Baseline variant = the current production `location` prompt section from
    18	    rhodesli_ml/gemini_extraction.py (unmodified).
    19	  - Candidate variant = three-round scaffold (describe → propose 2-3 candidates
    20	    → pick & eliminate). NO cheat-sheet city names pre-seeded. Same inputs
    21	    (image, optional metadata) as baseline.
    22	  - Both variants use the same model (GEMINI_MODEL), same temperature (0.1),
    23	    same response_mime_type.
    24	  - Logs every call to gemini_api_calls with experiment_id=session153_shadow_eval_<ts>.
    25	  - NO writes to date_labels, photos, identities, photo_faces.
    26	
    27	Cost cap: default $2.00 total, enforced by halting early if exceeded.
    28	
    29	Run:

docs/feedback/session-144-gemini-detroit-transcript.md:1:# Session 144 — Gemini Conversation Transcript: Detroit Identification
docs/feedback/session-144-gemini-detroit-transcript.md:5:**Subject photo:** `raw_photos/02068_p_13akf5twbc3600.jpg` (1918 three-sibling Fox photo)

docs/feedback/session-144-gemini-detroit-transcript.md:18:- `Anna Scripps Whitcomb Conservatory Belle Isle Detroit entrance`
docs/feedback/session-144-gemini-detroit-transcript.md:34:> Before I start, please make sure to always remember this and everything else I say. I have this picture of my Great Grandfather Albert (FKA Elia / Ellis) Fox. I believe his is with a couple of his siblings here (he was one of 11, 8 of whom survived to adulthood and had kids). He is the person on the bottom right hand corner of the group. He was born in Minsk on January 15, 1892 (that may be by the old calendar). He immigrated to New York by way of Antwerp on the Westernland arriving at New York on 10 May 1906 where his father was living at 67 Ludlow St in the Lower East Side. In the 1910 Census he was living at 328 Madison St in New York. When he did his Naturalization Declaration on 16 Jun 1911 his address was 53 Rutgers St in New York. In 1917 he was working in Detroit living at 74 (or maybe 76) Delmar St. In draft card from June 5, 1917 he list his Delmar St. address in Detroit along with two address in Brooklyn where his family was at the time (216 Marcy Ave and 32 Seigel St). He was Naturalized in Camp Devens in June of 1917 while he was in the service. He arrived back from France to his Seigel St address on 28 Apr 1919. He was married in Hamilton County, Ohio to my Great Grandmother Esther Burd. They both put their address as 1256 Gilsey Ave in Cincinnati, Ohio on the marriage license. By 1923 they were living in Dayton, Ohio where he lived for most of his life. By the 1910 census, Albert's brother Harry and his sister Bessie were living in Dayton, Ohio at 1410 E 5th St. Hence it is quite probable, given that Albert was in the Midwest in Detroit prior to serving World War 1, that he was in Ohio at some point before the war. Turning back to the picture, I want you to estimate how old he is in the photo, and then I want you to try to determine where the background of the picture is/where this was taken. The building behind him and his siblings looks very unique so I was hoping to identify it. Also to make things a bit easier, I'll include a photo from around when Albert got married to help you guess his age better.
docs/feedback/session-144-gemini-detroit-transcript.md:42:- **Best estimate: 20-25 years old, placing Image 2 between 1912 and 1917.**
docs/feedback/session-144-gemini-detroit-transcript.md:43:- **Geography heuristic:** Given Harry and Bessie were in Dayton by 1910, and Albert himself was in Detroit in 1917, the photo could plausibly be a Midwest landmark.
docs/feedback/session-144-gemini-detroit-transcript.md:45:- Offered to search further and compare against historical buildings in Ohio and Detroit. Asked for permission to proceed.
docs/feedback/session-144-gemini-detroit-transcript.md:55:- Verdict: **Anna Scripps Whitcomb Conservatory (Belle Isle Conservatory), Detroit, Michigan**, with high confidence.
docs/feedback/session-144-gemini-detroit-transcript.md:57:  1. **Biographical timeline:** Albert was in Detroit in 1917, matching the 1912-1917 age window.
docs/feedback/session-144-gemini-detroit-transcript.md:59:  3. **Historical context:** Conservatory designed by architect **Albert Kahn**, opened 1904 — so its appearance in a ~1917 photo would match modern photos closely.
docs/feedback/session-144-gemini-detroit-transcript.md:69:> - **Search local archives:** Detroit/Belle Isle archival collections and Dayton archives for the exact matching photo/postcard. Leads: HistoricDetroit, Burton Collection, Dayton postcard sellers.
docs/feedback/session-144-gemini-detroit-transcript.md:89:**Final conclusion:** virtually certain the photo was taken at the Anna Scripps Whitcomb Conservatory on Belle Isle, Detroit, around 1917.

The biographical evidence (Albert Fox's RESI Detroit 1917 and 1917-1918 single, EVEN 7 Jun 1918 draft induction, EVEN 5 Jul 1918 NY Camp Devens) localizes the only-Detroit-pre-induction window to 1917-mid-1918.

    A triage-focused interface for identity verification with epistemic humility.
    The UI reflects backend state - it never calculates probabilities.
    
    Error Semantics:
    - 409 = Variance Explosion (faces too dissimilar)
    - 423 = Lock Contention (another process is writing)
    - 404 = Identity or face not found

    # When running `python app/main.py`, sys.path[0] = app/ not project root.
    from pathlib import Path as _PathEarly
    
    _project_root = str(_PathEarly(__file__).resolve().parent.parent)
    if _project_root not in sys.path:
        sys.path.insert(0, _project_root)

    # (not a duplicate). Without this, compare_routes would get a different rt/app object.
    if "app.main" not in sys.modules:

        # Also ensure 'app' package is importable

    """
    Rhodesli Forensic Workstation.
    
    A triage-focused interface for identity verification with epistemic humility.
    The UI reflects backend state - it never calculates probabilities.
    
    Error Semantics:
    - 409 = Variance Explosion (faces too dissimilar)
    - 423 = Lock Contention (another process is writing)
    - 404 = Identity or face not found
    """
    
    import hashlib
    import heapq
    import json
    import logging
    import os
    import sys
    from contextlib import asynccontextmanager
    
    # Ensure project root is on sys.path for cross-module imports (compare_routes, estimate_routes, upload_routes).
    # When running `python app/main.py`, sys.path[0] = app/ not project root.
    from pathlib import Path as _PathEarly
    
    _project_root = str(_PathEarly(__file__).resolve().parent.parent)
    if _project_root not in sys.path:
        sys.path.insert(0, _project_root)
    # When running as __main__ (python app/main.py), register this module as 'app.main'
    # so that `from app.main import rt` in route modules gets the SAME module instance
    # (not a duplicate). Without this, compare_routes would get a different rt/app object.
    if "app.main" not in sys.modules:
        sys.modules["app.main"] = sys.modules[__name__]
        # Also ensure 'app' package is importable
        if "app" not in sys.modules:
            import types
    
            _app_pkg = types.ModuleType("app")
            _app_pkg.__path__ = [str(_PathEarly(__file__).resolve().parent)]
            sys.modules["app"] = _app_pkg
    if (
        not os.environ.get("RAILWAY_ENVIRONMENT")
        and "pytest" not in sys.modules
        and not os.environ.get("RHODESLI_SKIP_DOTENV")
    ):
        from dotenv import load_dotenv
    
        load_dotenv()
    import re
    import sys
    import time
    from datetime import datetime, timezone
    from pathlib import Path
    from urllib.parse import quote
    
    import numpy as np

    # (not a duplicate). Without this, compare_routes would get a different rt/app object.
    if "app.main" not in sys.modules:
        sys.modules["app.main"] = sys.modules[__name__]
        # Also ensure 'app' package is importable
        if "app" not in sys.modules:
            import types
    
            _app_pkg = types.ModuleType("app")

**Findings**

- **P1: Round 2.5 cannot rescue an omitted Detroit candidate.**  
  [scripts/session153_shadow_eval.py](/Users/nolanfox/rhodesli/.claude/worktrees/s167-detroit-fix/scripts/session153_shadow_eval.py:217) proposes 2-3 locations first, then [line 231](/Users/nolanfox/rhodesli/.claude/worktrees/s167-detroit-fix/scripts/session153_shadow_eval.py:231) only scores those candidates. If the model omits Detroit, the GEDCOM tie-breaker never applies.  
  **Fix:** require an explicit pre-table extraction of all qualifying GEDCOM places within the date window, and force any place with `year_distance <= 5` into `candidates[]` / `residence_distance_table`.

- **P1: The sycophancy guard is prompt-only, not confabulation-proof.**  
  The schema has no fields for `prior_decision`, `confirming_event`, `prior_year_distance`, or `round_2_5_winner` ([schema starts here](/Users/nolanfox/rhodesli/.claude/worktrees/s167-detroit-fix/scripts/session153_shadow_eval.py:330)), and `evaluate_result` does not verify any of the guard claims ([line 721](/Users/nolanfox/rhodesli/.claude/worktrees/s167-detroit-fix/scripts/session153_shadow_eval.py:721)). A model can invent or miscopy a named event and still be graded.  
  **Fix:** add structured prior-crosscheck fields and mechanically validate exact citation substring in supplied GEDCOM context, numeric `year_distance <= 5`, and winner consistency.

- **P1: Default provenance still clobbers/misattributes Session 167.**  
  Default `experiment_id` remains `session154_shadow_eval_<ts>` ([line 955](/Users/nolanfox/rhodesli/.claude/worktrees/s167-detroit-fix/scripts/session153_shadow_eval.py:955)), and Detroit-only output still overwrites `docs/feedback/session-154-shadow-eval-detroit-rerun.json` ([line 1177](/Users/nolanfox/rhodesli/.claude/worktrees/s167-detroit-fix/scripts/session153_shadow_eval.py:1177)). The new flags help only if remembered.  
  **Fix:** derive defaults from a required/auto session label, include timestamp or experiment id in the output filename, and refuse overwrite unless `--overwrite`.

- **P2: Date selection is gameable.**  
  `year_distance` depends on the model’s own estimated photo date ([line 256](/Users/nolanfox/rhodesli/.claude/worktrees/s167-detroit-fix/scripts/session153_shadow_eval.py:256)). On 02068, shifting the photo year can make Brooklyn look tied or superior.  
  **Fix:** provide a fixed eval `photo_year` / date window from metadata, or require the model to output `estimated_photo_year` and have the grader recompute the table.

- **P2: `evaluate_result` can mask wrong primaries.**  
  A wrong primary with Detroit buried in candidates returns `candidate_has_answer` ([line 758](/Users/nolanfox/rhodesli/.claude/worktrees/s167-detroit-fix/scripts/session153_shadow_eval.py:758)), which is easy to misread as partial success. Alias matching is also raw substring matching ([line 736](/Users/nolanfox/rhodesli/.claude/worktrees/s167-detroit-fix/scripts/session153_shadow_eval.py:736)).  
  **Fix:** add explicit `primary_correct` / `detroit_gate_pass = top1 && confidence in {medium,high}` and normalize aliases with word boundaries/canonical place ids.

- **P2: Tests are mostly string-presence checks.**  
  The new tests prove phrases exist, not that the prompt is unambiguous or the guard is enforceable. They do not test output-path defaults, experiment-id defaults, self-inconsistent Round 2.5 tables, hallucinated citations, or high-confidence violations.  
  **Fix:** add behavioral tests around `evaluate_result`/a validator using synthetic parsed outputs for: invented GEDCOM citation, wrong Round 2.5 winner, `year_distance > 5`, missing Detroit candidate, and stale default output path.

- **P3: Call provenance counters are inconsistent.**  
  `n_calls` writes `len(results)` ([line 1191](/Users/nolanfox/rhodesli/.claude/worktrees/s167-detroit-fix/scripts/session153_shadow_eval.py:1191)), not `call_count`, so silent first passes are undercounted in variant-only runs.  
  **Fix:** write both `n_result_rows` and `n_gemini_calls`.

No P0 found. I tried running `tests/test_shadow_eval_prompt_structure.py`; repo `venv` is absent, and ambient `python3` fails before tests on missing `fasthtml`, so I could not verify the suite in this environment.
